# C18: Flow feature catalogue

Researched 2026-10-31. Every feature is judged only against the description of Flow given in the request. Urbanto Studio's source was not seen. The machine-readable copy of the master table is [C18-flow-feature-catalogue.csv](C18-flow-feature-catalogue.csv).

## Answer

All five changes that would make Flow easiest for a new user fix the same gap. A newcomer cannot see what exists, what a node needs, what went wrong, or which node drew which shape.

1. **Start from something that already works.** Add a template gallery on New with urban starter flows, a start page, and empty-canvas hints that lead with Recording ([LIB-01](#lib-01), [LEARN-08](#learn-08), [LEARN-06](#learn-06)). ComfyUI built its App Mode because node graphs are "notorious for being difficult for beginners" ([Gigazine, 2026, third party](https://www.gigazine.net/gsc_news/en/20260312-comfyui-app-mode)).
2. **Let users browse and understand nodes before wiring them.** This means:
   - a library panel with a "Start here" set;
   - search results that say what a node does;
   - port tooltips;
   - marked required inputs;
   - an F1 help panel.

   These are [FIND-03](#find-03), [FIND-05](#find-05), [FIND-04](#find-04), [PORT-02](#port-02), [PORT-03](#port-03) and [LEARN-01](#learn-01). Dynamo's primer calls browsing the library "the best way to discover new Nodes" ([Dynamo Primer](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/3_user_interface/2-library)). When Blender 4.0 hid its search field, users answered "panic, where has the search box gone?" ([Blender Artists, 2023, third party](https://blenderartists.org/t/searching/1484515)).
3. **Make every problem say what to do.** Show error and warning states on nodes, write plain-language messages with fix buttons, give a reason when a wire is refused, and roll warnings up into groups and sub-flows ([STATE-01](#state-01), [STATE-02](#state-02), [WIRE-02](#wire-02), [STATE-06](#state-06)). Sverchok's own documentation admits raw errors help "sometimes not" ([Sverchok docs](https://nortikin.github.io/sverchok/docs/tree_evaluation_system.html)). Dynamo collected its 30 most common errors from its forum and issue tracker to explain them ([Dynamo 2.6, 2020, old](https://dynamobim.org/dynamo-core-2-6-release/)).
4. **Show what each node does in the drawing.** This means:
   - a viewer that pins the existing hover peek;
   - a preview eye on each node, with only end-of-chain results shown by default;
   - highlighting of the selected node's results;
   - picking a shape to jump to the node that made it.

   These are [DATA-02](#data-02), [PREV-03](#prev-03), [PREV-04](#prev-04) and [PREV-09](#prev-09). Grasshopper users have asked for the last one since 2021 ([McNeel forum, 2021](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-ui-wishlist/122573)).
5. **Let people use a flow without wiring it.** Allow values to be edited on the node, any value to be exposed as a tool input, and a Player to run a library flow from a form, as [Dynamo Player](https://help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2026/ENU/Revit-HaveYouTried/files/GUID-E8205058-9CA4-4CFF-95F5-A6AA1CDA7346.htm) does ([PORT-01](#port-01), [PARAM-03](#param-03), [PARAM-01](#param-01)).

These five changes cover 20 catalogue features. Ten are size S (hours) and ten are size M (days); none is size L. Twelve complete things Flow already has: the hover peek, the Show pills, the Made-by badge, flows as tools and the library folder. So most of the gain comes from finishing Flow, not from new machinery. The catalogue lists 195 features: 34 EXISTS, 70 PARTLY and 91 MISSING.

## How to read this catalogue

Each feature has one paragraph in the group sections below and one row in the master table at the end. Every paragraph covers, in this order:
- what the feature is;
- which studied programs have it, each linked;
- why users want it, linked, with old or third-party sources flagged inline;
- what the Studio version would be;
- a closing line with Status, Size, Value, Kind and Depends on.

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Status | EXISTS: the Flow description states it fully. PARTLY: the description has a foundation or part of it; the quoted words say which. MISSING: nothing in the description. |
| Size | S = hours (up to about a day), M = days (2 to 10 working days), L = weeks (more than two weeks), for the Studio version described. EXISTS rows have no size. |
| Value | Value to an urban designer, 1 to 5, weighted towards new users. 5 removes a blocker for new users or is used in nearly every urban-design flow. 4 is a large, frequent win. 3 is useful regularly. 2 is for power users or occasional use. 1 rarely matters. |
| Kind | COMPLETION finishes or extends something Flow already has. NEW is a new capability. |
| Value/size | Value divided by a size weight of S = 1, M = 2, L = 4. This is the sort key of the master table. |

Size and value are judgements made with this rubric. They are not measured numbers. Every number quoted inside a paragraph has its own link.

| Group | Features | EXISTS | PARTLY | MISSING |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Finding and adding nodes (FIND) | 13 | 3 | 4 | 6 |
| Wiring (WIRE) | 13 | 4 | 0 | 9 |
| Ports, values and inline editing (PORT) | 11 | 1 | 3 | 7 |
| Node state, errors and debugging (STATE) | 13 | 2 | 1 | 10 |
| Data shapes (DATA) | 9 | 0 | 3 | 6 |
| Containers, groups, frames and sub-flows (CONT) | 14 | 5 | 8 | 1 |
| Navigation and large graphs (NAV) | 14 | 4 | 2 | 8 |
| Previews in the drawing and inspection (PREV) | 11 | 3 | 4 | 4 |
| Parameters and user interfaces (PARAM) | 14 | 2 | 7 | 5 |
| Templates, snippets, favourites and libraries (LIB) | 10 | 2 | 5 | 3 |
| Collaboration and sharing (COLLAB) | 13 | 1 | 3 | 9 |
| Learning and help (LEARN) | 10 | 1 | 3 | 6 |
| Performance and feedback (PERF) | 14 | 2 | 8 | 4 |
| Scripting and extension (SCRIPT) | 14 | 2 | 7 | 5 |
| CAD integration (CAD) | 12 | 1 | 8 | 3 |
| Automation (AUTO) | 10 | 1 | 4 | 5 |
| **Total** | **195** | **34** | **70** | **91** |

## Feature catalogue

### Finding and adding nodes (FIND)

13 features: 3 EXISTS, 4 PARTLY, 6 MISSING.

<a id="find-01"></a>

#### FIND-01 Search at the cursor
A type-to-find box that opens where the pointer is, ranks nodes by name, keywords and synonyms, and places the pick there. Most editors ship it: the [Houdini Tab menu](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/nodes.html), the [Nuke Tab menu](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/tab_menu.html) with partial strings and capitals, [Blender 4.0 Add menus](https://developer.blender.org/docs/release_notes/4.0/user_interface/) searched by typing, the [Grasshopper double-click popup](https://grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/popup-shortcuts) (2013, old), [ComfyUI double-click search](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/shortcuts), the [Unity Shader Graph Create Node menu](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Create-Node-Menu.html) with industry synonyms, the [TouchDesigner OP Create Dialog](https://docs.derivative.ca/OP_Create_Dialog) and [Enso aliases](https://help.enso.org/docs/getting-started/enso-graph-editor) ("Where" finds Filter). Users who know it love it: "It's just so handy to just type" ([Blender Artists, 2023, third party](https://blenderartists.org/t/searching/1484515)). But a Blender module meeting heard that "New users wont know that hit space bar will start to search" ([devtalk, 2023](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/2023-10-10-user-interface-meeting/31324/20)). Flow already opens search from Tab, Space or double-click, fuzzy on name, keywords and tags; the only addition worth making is a visible "Search nodes" hint on an empty canvas and urban-design synonyms in the keywords (setback, plot, footprint).
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="find-02"></a>

#### FIND-02 Wire-drop search filtered to compatible nodes
Dragging a wire into empty space opens search showing only nodes that can take (or feed) that wire, and wires the pick. It is in [Blender 3.1](https://developer.blender.org/docs/release_notes/3.1/nodes_physics/) ("a new search menu allows quickly connecting a node with a compatible socket"), the [Unreal Blueprint "Filtered Action Menu for Pin"](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-editor-cheat-sheet-in-unreal-engine), [Unity Shader Graph](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Create-Node-Menu.html), [ComfyUI link release](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph) and [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) (click a connector, then Tab). A new Grasshopper user's first wiring wish was exactly this menu ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)), and Dynamo's team built its version to "Lower the Barrier to Entry" by limiting new users to viable nodes ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/feature-preview-node-autocomplete/55768)). Flow already does this: a wire dropped on empty canvas opens a search filtered to compatible nodes and wires the result. Ranking and suggestions on top of it are FIND-07.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="find-03"></a>

#### FIND-03 Browsable node library panel
A dockable panel listing every node by category, with a filter box, hover details and drag-to-canvas, so users can browse instead of guessing names. The [Dynamo Library](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/3_user_interface/2-library) groups nodes into categories with Create, Action and Query; the [ComfyUI Node Library](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/node-library) has All nodes, bookmarks and drag; the [Unreal Material Editor Palette](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-engine-material-editor-ui) is a categorised drag list; the [Node-RED palette](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/palette/) has collapsible categories and a filter; the [TouchDesigner OP Create Dialog](https://docs.derivative.ca/OP_Create_Dialog) shows families as headings. The Dynamo primer calls browsing "the best way to discover new Nodes you haven't used before" ([primer](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/3_user_interface/2-library)); when Blender hid its search field, users answered "panic, where has the search box gone?" and "Reduced discoverability" ([Blender Artists, 2023, third party](https://blenderartists.org/t/searching/1484515)). Flow would add a Library pane inside the flow window, grouped by the program's own command categories plus Sources, Values, Expressions, Containers and library flows, with a filter, drag onto canvas or wire, and FIND-04 details on hover.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** the search index (name, keywords, tags); FIND-04 for details; Flow library folder for library flows

<a id="find-04"></a>

#### FIND-04 Search results that show what a node does
Each search or library result shows a plain description, its inputs and outputs with types, and a small preview before the user commits. [Dynamo's library hover](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/3_user_interface/2-library) shows a description, inputs with name, data type and structure, and outputs; [ComfyUI's search box](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/comfy) shows a node preview and category by default (added in [frontend v1.1.0](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI_frontend/main/README.md)); [Dynamo 3.6](https://dynamobim.org/?p=56190) shows the suggested node "ghosted" with its wire before placing. In Dynamo's autocomplete feedback a user wrote that "it is not easy for a beginner to undestand what is a combinator and which nodes are valid" ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/request-for-feedback-dynamo-node-autocomplete/57246)). Flow would add a detail pane beside the result list: name, one-line description, ports with type, stroke (one item, list, list of lists) and unit, and a thumbnail of typical output in the drawing; arrow keys browse, Enter places. Commands already declare their inputs, so ports come for free; descriptions need writing once per command.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (search at the cursor) · **Depends on:** a description per command; PORT-02 (same text)

<a id="find-05"></a>

#### FIND-05 Starter set for new users
A short, curated set of the nodes most flows need, shown first when the library or an empty search opens. [ComfyUI's Essentials tab](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/node-library) is "a curated grid of the nodes most workflows need, grouped by task", and [Node-RED's quick-add dialog](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/nodes) "shows the five main core nodes at the top of the list". The need comes from the same discoverability gap: Blender's own meeting notes say new users do not know where search starts ([devtalk, 2023](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/2023-10-10-user-interface-meeting/31324/20)), and Dynamo's team describes the alternative as "searching through the library of hundreds or even thousands of nodes" ([Dynamo 2.17 release, 2023](https://dynamobim.org/?p=53951)). Flow would add a "Start here" tab in the Library and the empty-search state, holding about twenty items grouped by urban-design task (Sources: layer, selection, spreadsheet range; Values: number, slider, choice; measure, offset, array, for-each, Bake), with the list kept as an editable file in the library folder so an office can set its own.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** FIND-03

<a id="find-06"></a>

#### FIND-06 Add after the selected node and wire it
With a node selected, a node added from search lands downstream of it and is wired to the first compatible port, so chains are typed rather than dragged. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/nodes.html) wires a new node into the current node with Shift+Enter; [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/connecting_nodes.html) "automatically generates pipes between the currently selected node and the new nodes"; [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/wires) chains new nodes with Ctrl-click; [TouchDesigner](https://docs.derivative.ca/OP_Create_Dialog) wires several operators together when Shift is held. Dynamo's team named the slow path it wanted to remove: "right-click, search for node, select node, place node, wire up nodes, align nodes" ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/feature-preview-node-autocomplete/55768)). Flow would do this when exactly one node is selected as search opens: place the result to its right, wire the first type-compatible output to input, select the new node so the user keeps typing, and record it as one canvas-history step; Shift+Enter places unwired.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (search at the cursor) · **Depends on:** the compatibility matching behind wire-drop search; tidy

<a id="find-07"></a>

#### FIND-07 Ranked "suggest next node"
From any port, a short list of the nodes most likely to come next, ranked by how often nodes follow each other rather than by type alone. [Dynamo Node Autocomplete](https://help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2026/ENU/RevitDynamo/files/RevitDynamo_Node_Autocomplete_html.html) offers "Recommended Nodes" (machine learning, with confidence percentages) or "Node Type Match"; [Dynamo 2.17](https://dynamobim.org/?p=53951) added the ranking and [Dynamo 3.6](https://dynamobim.org/?p=56190) an icon on port hover; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/comfy) shows a recommendation list on link release (5 suggestions by default); [Enso](https://help.enso.org/docs/getting-started/enso-graph-editor) offers relevant components from a "+" under each component. Users called it "a feature all beginners have been asking for" ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/request-for-feedback-dynamo-node-autocomplete/57246)), yet Dynamo admits it was "a bit hidden behind two clicks" ([3.6 release, 2025](https://dynamobim.org/?p=56190)). Flow would keep its type-filtered wire-drop search and add a "+" handle on port hover, ranking by counts taken locally from the user's and the office library's flows (no upload), showing the top five with a ghost preview.
**Status:** PARTLY: "a wire dropped on empty canvas opens a search filtered to compatible nodes and wires the result" · **Size:** M · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (wire-drop search) · **Depends on:** Flow library folder (.urbflow) for statistics; FIND-04

<a id="find-08"></a>

#### FIND-08 Recent and favourite nodes
Recently used and starred nodes appear first in search and in the library. [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/tab_menu.html) pins starred favourites to the top and weights often-added nodes, while warning that weighting "can produce unexpected results"; [Blender 4.0](https://developer.blender.org/docs/release_notes/4.0/user_interface/) puts recently searched items at the top; [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/nodes) lists recently added nodes; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/node-library) has a Bookmarked section. Unreal users report favourites becoming "a long unordered list" ([Unreal forum, 2022, third party](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/categories-for-blueprint-palette-favorites/717412)), and a 2026 plugin adds a hotkey favourites menu "no digging through the node palette" ([Unreal forum, 2026, third party](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/boundless-bite-favorite-nodes/2742144)). Flow would show Recent (last eight) and Starred groups in the empty search and Library, a star toggle on every result, per-user storage, and a capped boost so an exact name match always ranks first.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (search at the cursor) · **Depends on:** FIND-03

<a id="find-09"></a>

#### FIND-09 Typed values become source nodes
Typing a value instead of a name creates a value node directly. The [Grasshopper popup](https://grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/popup-shortcuts) (2013, old) makes a slider from a number, a text panel from a leading double quote or "//", and a point from "#,#"; in [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/objects/) the n key opens an object box with autocomplete where the user types. Users asked for these shortcuts to be documented as early as 2012 ("like typing "//text" creates a panel component") ([Grasshopper forum, 2012, old](https://www.grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/what-hotkeys-and-shortcuts-are-available-in-grasshopper)). Flow already turns typed numbers and quoted text into source nodes; richer syntax that also sets ranges and first inputs is FIND-10.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="find-10"></a>

#### FIND-10 Quick-add with initial values and ranges
A small syntax in the search box that picks a node and sets values at once: a slider range, a first input, or an expression. The [Grasshopper popup](https://grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/popup-shortcuts) (2013, old) accepts "Offset=5" ("initialisation codes"), "0<5<10" or "0..2..10" for sliders with limits and decimals, and "f(x,y)=x*y" for an expression; a [Max object box](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/objects/) takes arguments after the name to set initial state. Dynamo's team describes the same goal: placing a node with "Sheet1" already in it "instead of having to ask for the most likely node and then fill out "Sheet1"" ([Dynamo forum, 2025](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/is-autodesk-copying-every-dynamo-script-we-make/108513)). Flow would extend its typed numbers and quoted text: "2..10" makes a slider, "Offset 5 m" places Offset with distance 5 m in its inline field, "= x*2" makes an expression node, with a live hint under the box saying what Enter will create.
**Status:** PARTLY: "typed numbers and quoted text become source nodes" · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (typed values in search) · **Depends on:** PORT-01; PORT-05 for units

<a id="find-11"></a>

#### FIND-11 Insert from search into a selected wire
Select a wire, open search, and the chosen node is spliced into that wire, without dragging. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html): "Select the wire, press Tab, and then use the menu to choose the type of node to insert"; [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/nodes): "If a wire is clicked on when triggering the dialog, the added node will be spliced into the wire"; [TouchDesigner](https://docs.derivative.ca/OP_Create_Dialog) has "Add Operator on a Wire". A new Grasshopper user listed "The ability to put a component in between a wire" ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)), and a Blender proposal shows drag-splicing in crowded graphs causing accidental connections and slow recomputes ([devtalk, 2022](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/proposal-addressing-geometry-nodes-ux-issues/23239)), which an explicit insert avoids. Flow would let a click select a wire; Tab then opens search filtered to nodes whose input and output both fit, and Enter splices as one history step.
**Status:** PARTLY: "a node dropped on a wire splices in after a pause" · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (splice on wire) · **Depends on:** selectable wires; FIND-12 type matching

<a id="find-12"></a>

#### FIND-12 Filter search by data type or category
Chips or prefixes that narrow search by input type, output type, category or source. [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/placing-nodes-in-unreal-engine) has a Context Sensitive checkbox that filters the menu; [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/tab_menu.html) searches by toolbar menu name, for example "[Color]"; [Dynamo's Node Type Match](https://help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2026/ENU/RevitDynamo/files/RevitDynamo_Node_Autocomplete_html.html) ranks by port type; [ComfyUI's library](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/node-library) filters by source (core, partner, extensions, blueprints). In Dynamo's autocomplete feedback a user wrote "Best idea would be to search through the library looking at the output type" ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/request-for-feedback-dynamo-node-autocomplete/57246)). Flow would add chips under the search box (Takes: curve; Gives: area; Category: measure; From: library flows) and typed prefixes such as "out:area", reusing the type filter that wire-drop search already applies.
**Status:** PARTLY: "a wire dropped on empty canvas opens a search filtered to compatible nodes" · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (search at the cursor) · **Depends on:** typed port declarations; PORT-05 for quantity types

<a id="find-13"></a>

#### FIND-13 Hotkeys for frequent nodes
Hold a key and click to drop a frequent node without opening search. [Unreal Blueprints](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-editor-cheat-sheet-in-unreal-engine) place Branch with B+click and Sequence with S+click; the [Material Editor](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-engine-material-editor-ui) places Constant with 1+click and Multiply with M+click; [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/tab_menu.html) adds Blur with B; [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patching/) creates an object box with n; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/shortcuts) lets every command be rebound. Grasshopper users personalise component shortcuts by right-clicking toolbar icons ([Grasshopper forum, 2012, old](https://www.grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/what-hotkeys-and-shortcuts-are-available-in-grasshopper)), and a 2026 Unreal plugin sells hotkey favourites ([Unreal forum, 2026, third party](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/boundless-bite-favorite-nodes/2742144)). Flow would offer user-assignable hold-key-plus-click placement (for example hold N for Number, L for Layer source), shown on library cards; holding avoids clashes with Flow's single keys D, B and H. Mainly a power-user speed-up.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 2 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** FIND-03 (to show bindings)

### Wiring (WIRE)

13 features: 4 EXISTS, 0 PARTLY, 9 MISSING.

<a id="wire-01"></a>

#### WIRE-01 Typed, coloured wires with structure strokes
Wires and ports coloured by data type, with a stroke or shape showing whether one item, a list or a nested list flows. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/parts.html) colours sockets by type and shapes them by structure (single rectangle, field diamond, list three lines); [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patch_cords/) distinguishes six kinds of patch cord "by their stripe patterns and colors"; [Unity Shader Graph](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Data-Types.html) and [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/links) colour ports and wires per type ("You can only connect ports of the same color"). ComfyUI's docs argue strong typing "is a huge benefit to users, guiding them to proper workflow construction" ([vendor docs](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/properties)). Flow already has typed wires coloured by type, with strokes for one item, a list, a list of lists and a lazy output; WIRE-02 and WIRE-03 build on it.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="wire-02"></a>

#### WIRE-02 Say why a connection is refused
While a wire is dragged, compatible ports light up and an incompatible one says why it cannot connect, ideally with a fix. [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/connecting-nodes-in-unreal-engine): "A green check mark will appear when hovering over a compatible pin" and otherwise "an icon will appear along with the reason why you cannot connect"; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph) highlights connectable nodes and snaps to a suitable slot; [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) draws the wire dashed until it snaps solid. Dynamo's team wanted to "alleviate a lot of error nodal miss-connections" by new users ([Dynamo forum, 2020, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/feature-preview-node-autocomplete/55768)), and a Blender proposal describes users forced into "trial and error" by unexplained connection behaviour ([devtalk, 2022](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/proposal-addressing-geometry-nodes-ux-issues/23239)). Flow would glow compatible ports, dim the rest, and on hover over a refused port show one line ("needs a curve; this is a list of numbers") with a one-click fix when one exists (insert For-each, Flatten or a conversion).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (typed wires) · **Depends on:** WIRE-03 for fixes; PORT-05 for unit mismatches

<a id="wire-03"></a>

#### WIRE-03 Visible automatic conversion on type mismatch
When two different but convertible types are joined, the editor converts automatically and shows that it did. [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/connecting-nodes-in-unreal-engine) inserts a Conversion Node between, for example, an integer output and a string input; [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/parts.html) converts float, vector, colour and boolean implicitly and offers an [Implicit Conversion node](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/utilities/implicit_conversion.html) to make it visible; [Unity Shader Graph](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Data-Types.html) promotes or truncates vectors. Beginners hit "Data conversion failed from Text to Integer" ([McNeel forum, 2023](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/data-conversion-failed-from-text-to-integer/154859)), and Blender warns that a plain value plugged into an angle socket is read as radians "regardless of the scene's Units" ([vendor docs](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/parts.html)). Flow would place a small conversion chip on the wire (text to number, polyline to curve, single item to list), click to read the rule or expand it into a node, and never convert silently when data would be lost.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** WIRE-02; PORT-05

<a id="wire-04"></a>

#### WIRE-04 Highlight a node's wires and follow them
Hovering a port or selecting a node brightens its wires and fades the rest, so connections can be traced in a busy flow. [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-editor-cheat-sheet-in-unreal-engine) lists "Emphasize Connected Wires" on "Mouse Hover over Pin"; [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) renders a selected node's wires in its highlight colour; [Grasshopper](https://developer.rhino3d.com/api/grasshopper/html/T_Grasshopper_Kernel_GH_ParamWireDisplay.htm) shows faint or hidden wires fully only while an end is selected, and Ctrl+arrow keys highlight upstream or downstream ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/wish-highlight-the-other-end-of-selected-wire/106151)). The same thread's request: neighbours "are difficult to identify sometimes because of wire overlay". Flow would brighten the wires of a hovered port or selected node, halo the nodes at the far ends, and let the existing arrow-key navigation jump along the highlighted wire with Ctrl+Left and Ctrl+Right.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** arrows between nodes (keyboard)

<a id="wire-05"></a>

#### WIRE-05 Drop a node on a wire to splice it
Dropping a node onto a wire inserts it between the two ends. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/arranging.html) inserts and auto-offsets neighbours; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/nodes.html), [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/connecting_nodes.html) and [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/wires) splice on drop; [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patch_cords/) does it with Shift-drag. A new Grasshopper user asked for it ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)), while a Blender proposal shows the risk: in crowded graphs nodes get spliced by accident and trigger "heavy, expensive to compute operations" ([devtalk, 2022](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/proposal-addressing-geometry-nodes-ux-issues/23239)). Flow already splices a dropped node after a pause, which addresses that risk; FIND-11 adds an explicit insert from search.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="wire-06"></a>

#### WIRE-06 Delete a node and reconnect around it
Deleting a node joins its upstream and downstream wires so the chain stays whole. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) has Delete with Reconnect (Ctrl-Delete); [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/nodes.html) "tries to fix itself by connecting all the descendants of the deleted node to the node's first parent"; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph) has "Keep all links when deleting nodes". A new Grasshopper user asked for "A keyboard combination to delete a component while rewiring the wires" ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)). Flow already has Ctrl+Delete to delete and reconnect.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="wire-07"></a>

#### WIRE-07 Cut wires with a stroke
A modifier-drag draws a line, and every wire it crosses is cut. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) cuts with Ctrl-RMB; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) cuts while Y is held; [Grasshopper](https://www.grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/what-hotkeys-and-shortcuts-are-available-in-grasshopper) erases a wire by tracing over it with Ctrl (2012, old). A Grasshopper user in 2026 calls disconnecting wires one of "two features that I use frequently" and is working on a way to put it on a hotkey ([McNeel forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/shortcut-for-hidden-wires-and-disconnect/220991)), which shows how often the gesture is used in real flows. Flow already cuts wires with Alt-drag across them; the only refinement worth considering is showing the cut line and the wires it will remove before the button is released.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="wire-08"></a>

#### WIRE-08 Move, copy and swap wires with modifier drags
Modifier keys while dragging from a port move an existing wire, move all wires of a port, add a wire without replacing, or swap two. [Grasshopper](https://www.grasshopper3d.com/forum/topics/what-hotkeys-and-shortcuts-are-available-in-grasshopper) uses Shift to add, Ctrl to erase and Ctrl+Shift to "Move all existing wires to some other grip" (2012, old); [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/connecting-nodes-in-unreal-engine) moves all connections with Ctrl-drag; [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) swaps links with Alt and repositions outgoing links with Ctrl; [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) reconnects several with Shift and duplicates with Ctrl; [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/wires) moves all wires with Shift. A Blender proposal shows why the gestures must be explicit: hidden automatic reconnection left users unable "to anticipate the behavior" ([devtalk, 2022](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/proposal-addressing-geometry-nodes-ux-issues/23239)). Flow would let a drag from a connected input move that wire, Ctrl-drag move all wires of a port, Shift-drag add without replacing, with the status bar naming the active gesture; Alt stays cut.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (Alt-drag cuts wires) · **Depends on:** WIRE-10 for multi-wire inputs

<a id="wire-09"></a>

#### WIRE-09 Reroute points
Small points on a wire that bend it around nodes and can branch it. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/types/layout/reroute.html) adds a Reroute by Shift+RMB sweep; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) adds dots with Alt-click and can pin them; [Unreal](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/organizing-a-material-graph-in-unreal-engine) and [Unity Shader Graph](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Redirect-Node.html) add one by double-clicking a wire; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/links) has native reroutes; [Nuke](https://learn.foundry.com/nuke/content/getting_started/using_interface/connecting_nodes.html) has the Dot. Epic notes unorganized graphs are "difficult to comprehend" when handed to someone else ([vendor docs](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/organizing-a-material-graph-in-unreal-engine)); a new Grasshopper user wants "snaps for straight alignment of wires" ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)), and SnappingGecko exists as an alignment plug-in for it ([food4rhino page, 2018, old, third party; its download count is not quoted because the page returned HTTP 405 when re-checked on 2026-10-31](https://www.food4rhino.com/en/app/snappinggecko)). Flow would add a point on double-click of a wire, coloured by the wire's type, kept by tidy, saved in the .urbflow, and removed with Delete. Modo's Schematic adds an Elbow node by double-clicking a link or pressing the period key, because "Elbow nodes can be placed on a link to reduce visual clutter in complex scenes" ([Modo 17.1 docs](https://learn.foundry.com/modo/content/help/pages/animation/schematic_view.html)).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** tidy (layered layout) must respect points

<a id="wire-10"></a>

#### WIRE-10 Several wires into one input, in a visible order
An input that accepts several wires merges them in an order the user can see and change. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) draws multi-input sockets pill-shaped; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) lists a Merge node's inputs in the parameter editor for drag reordering and rotates them with Shift+R; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/features/autogrow-inputs) warns "Order matters" for growing inputs. Grasshopper users avoid Shift-dragging several wires into one input "because you have no visual clue of the order in which they were connected" ([McNeel forum, 2024](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/arc-shape-issues-at-the-both-ends/195749)) and asked for an input order manager ([McNeel forum, 2018, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/wish-list-wire-input-order-manager/67197)). Flow would let list-taking inputs accept several wires, show small numbers at the port for merge order, reorder by drag in a pop-over, and show the merged result in the port peek.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** WIRE-08 (Shift to add); list strokes on wires

<a id="wire-11"></a>

#### WIRE-11 Wire styles, fading and faint wires
Display options for wires: curved, straight or right-angled routing, fading of long wires, and per-wire faint display. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/options.html) has Wire Style (rounded, straight, subway-map) and "Fade Long Wires"; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph) offers Straight, Linear, Spline or Hidden links plus midpoint markers; [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patch_cords/) has segmented cords, Auto Align and Route; [Grasshopper](https://developer.rhino3d.com/api/grasshopper/html/T_Grasshopper_Kernel_GH_ParamWireDisplay.htm) sets Default, Faint or Hidden per input; [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) can hide all or individual wires. A 2026 Grasshopper thread wants a hotkey for hidden wires ([McNeel forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/shortcut-for-hidden-wires-and-disconnect/220991)), while ComfyUI advises showing links "when learning, sharing, or debugging" ([vendor docs](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/links)). Flow would add a per-flow style (curved, straight, orthogonal), fading for very long wires, and a per-wire "faint" that returns to full on selection; fully hidden wires are left to WIRE-12.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (wires typed and coloured with strokes) · **Depends on:** WIRE-04

<a id="wire-12"></a>

#### WIRE-12 Named wireless links
A named sender and one or more receivers carry data across the canvas without a drawn wire. [Unreal Named Reroutes](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/organizing-a-material-graph-in-unreal-engine) act "like a tunnel or portal"; [Max send/receive](https://docs.cycling74.com/reference/send/) offers "Show others" to list partners; the Grasshopper [Telepathy plugin](https://www.food4rhino.com/en/app/telepathy) (2017, old, third party; not re-verified on 2026-10-31, food4rhino returned HTTP 405) pairs senders and receivers by key. Telepathy's authors say "Complex definitions can quickly become a mess of wires", and a new Grasshopper user asked for send/receive ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)); but a Blender designer warns hidden links can turn flows into "clouds of randomly floating disconnected node rectangles" ([devtalk, 2021, old](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/portal-links-exploration/21306/62)). Flow would add a Send/Receive pair with a name, both ends showing name and type stroke, a dashed ghost wire on hover, "Go to sender/receivers" in the menu, and lineage passing through for "Explain this number". Telepathy's source shows how pairing works: a Remote Sender's nickname is its key, and setting it reconnects the matching Remote Receivers ([Telepathy on GitHub](https://github.com/andrewheumann/telepathy), [sender code](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/andrewheumann/telepathy/master/Telepathy/Param_RemoteSender.cs), third party), and its README says "Telepathy is no longer actively maintained". Cables.gl's variable ops (VarSetNumber, VarGetNumber) are, in its words, "numbers you can read anywhere in your patch without creating very long cables" ([cables.gl op docs](https://cables.gl/op/Ops.Vars.VarSetNumber_v2)). Grasshopper 2 now builds this in: "The Shout-Listen pattern in GH2 allows for the wireless sharing of values within and between files" ([McNeel, Grasshopper 2 page, undated](https://www.rhino3d.com/stories/grasshopper-2/)).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** WIRE-04; lineage

<a id="wire-13"></a>

#### WIRE-13 Connect many nodes at once
One gesture wires several selected nodes together, or one output to several inputs. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) has Make Links (J) for selected nodes; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) wires nodes by holding J and drawing across them, and connects all selected nodes when one is wired to a multi-input; [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/workspace/wires) keeps a new wire going from the destination while Ctrl is held, and [Max](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patch_cords/) starts a new cord from the same outlet when Shift is held. Node-RED users call wiring several outputs to one input "quite tedious" ([Node-RED forum, 2023](https://discourse.nodered.org/t/connect-one-or-multiple-selected-nodes-to-the-same-target-in-one-go/73662)) and ask to wire one source to many targets ([Node-RED forum, 2022](https://discourse.nodered.org/t/is-there-a-way-to-wire-a-source-node-to-multiple-other-nodes-without-constantly-dragging-between-source-and-target/57953)). Flow would connect selected nodes left to right by best type match with J, and let Shift held after a connection start a new wire from the same output.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 2 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** compatibility matching (WIRE-02)

### Ports, values and inline editing (PORT)

11 features: 1 EXISTS, 3 PARTLY, 7 MISSING.

<a id="port-01"></a>

#### PORT-01 Edit default values right on the node
Each unconnected input shows a small editable field on the node itself, so a value can be set without adding a value node. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/parts.html) gives every disconnected input "a default value which can be edited via a color, numeric, or vector interface input", and its [number fields](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/buttons/fields.html) drag, step and accept expressions; [Unity Shader Graph](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Port.html) shows a Default Input field; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/properties) widgets sit on nodes; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/parms.html) adds a value ladder; [Enso](https://help.enso.org/) promises "all the configuration shown at a glance". A Dynamo student's nodes stayed grey because a default value was not switched on ([Dynamo forum, 2021, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/exercise-goes-wrong/58997)). Flow would draw a compact field per unconnected input (number with drag-scrub, text, choice dropdown, toggle, colour, layer picker), hide it when a wire connects, and keep the property editor for long forms.
**Status:** PARTLY: "values (number, slider, text, choice, bool)" and "Enter to the property editor" · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (values, property editor) · **Depends on:** PORT-05 for units; PORT-08

<a id="port-02"></a>

#### PORT-02 Port tooltips with type, description and value
Hovering any port, input or output, shows its full name, what it means, its type and its current value. [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) shows port label, tooltip, data type and default value; [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) shows "a tooltip explaining the purpose of the input"; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph) shows parameter descriptions; [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/groups.html) group sockets carry a Description shown as tooltip, and [socket inspection](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/inspection.html) shows last values. A Blender user complains hovering usually "just gives you "Input value used for unconnected socket". That's not helpful" ([devtalk, 2019, old](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/blender-ui-paper-cuts/2596/3474)); a Grasshopper beginner wants "the full labels of inputs and outputs" ([McNeel forum, 2023](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/draw-full-names-component-exceptions-slider-names/166142)). Flow would extend its output hover peek to every port: name, plain description, type and stroke, unit, required or optional, and the current value or first items. Hovering the node itself would show its one-line description, as Dynamo's library shows a [plain-language description on hover](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/3_user_interface/2-library), because a learner wrote that "if you don't know which component to use, or even if a component exists, you are basically out of luck" ([forum thread, 2022](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/exercise-in-using-all-the-standard-grasshopper-components/137497/1)).
**Status:** PARTLY: "Output ports show count badges and a hover peek of the first items" · **Size:** S · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (hover peek) · **Depends on:** a description per input (shared with FIND-04)

<a id="port-03"></a>

#### PORT-03 Mark required and optional inputs
Inputs that must be filled look different from optional ones, and the node says what it still needs. [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires) draws "a red vertical bar over one or more input ports" for unsatisfied inputs and blue bars when satisfied, and lets a port's default value be switched on or off; [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/right-side-panel) lists missing inputs in an Errors tab. A Dynamo student copied an exercise exactly and it failed because nodes "still need input to work" ([Dynamo forum, 2021, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/exercise-goes-wrong/58997)). Flow commands already declare their inputs, so Flow would mark required ports with a filled marker, show optional ones with their default, write "needs: curve" on the node until satisfied, and in manual run list unfilled required inputs with a click to jump to each.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (commands declare their inputs) · **Depends on:** PORT-01 for defaults

<a id="port-04"></a>

#### PORT-04 Count badges and hover peek on outputs
Output ports show how many items they hold and a quick look at the first values. [Blender socket inspection](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/inspection.html) shows the value or, for geometry, element counts; [Max probing](https://docs.cycling74.com/userguide/patch_cords/) shows a patch cord's contents on hover. A Grasshopper expert advises beginners to "look carefully at the output of each component" and uses "temporary text panels for that" ([McNeel forum, 2022](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/project-and-solid-union-problems/137323)), work a peek removes. Flow already shows count badges and a hover peek of the first items on output ports; PORT-02 extends this to inputs.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="port-05"></a>

#### PORT-05 Units on ports
Ports declare a quantity (length, area, angle, percent) and show and accept values in the drawing's units. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/groups.html) socket subtypes such as Distance and Angle use the scene's units, and its [number fields](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/buttons/fields.html) accept "1m 3mm" or "2ft"; Dynamo treats "Length" as a data type ([Dynamo forum, 2014, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/unit-confusion/283)). The same Dynamo thread is titled "Unit Confusion" and staff reply "I agree that it's confusing"; Blender warns a unitless value plugged into an angle socket is read as radians ([vendor docs](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/parts.html)). Flow would let commands declare quantities on ports, show m, m² or degrees in inline fields and peeks, convert typed "12 ft", carry the quantity on wires, and refuse area-into-length with a reason.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** PORT-01; WIRE-02; WIRE-03

<a id="port-06"></a>

#### PORT-06 Extract an input into a value node, and back
One click turns an input into a separate value node of the right kind, already wired, and folds it back. [Grasshopper](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/extract-parameter-as-a-dropdown-list/100889) has "Extract parameter" on an input's right-click menu; [ComfyUI](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI_frontend/main/README.md) converts a widget to an input when a link of the right type is dropped on it (frontend v1.3.4), and its docs note "a property can be converted from widget to input" ([vendor docs](https://docs.comfy.org/basic-concepts/properties)); [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/groups.html) creates a group socket when a link is dragged to the Group Input's hollow socket. Grasshopper users ask that extracting a choice input give "a Value List component with words for the five options" instead of an integer ([McNeel forum, 2024](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/request-extract-parameter-should-give-a-value-list-component-for-enums/187277)). Flow would add "Make a value node" on an input: a slider with the port's range, a choice node prefilled with the command's options, or text, all wired; "Fold into node" returns it inline.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (values: number, slider, text, choice, bool) · **Depends on:** PORT-01

<a id="port-07"></a>

#### PORT-07 Full names and plain labels
Nodes and ports show full, readable names instead of nicknames or icons only. Grasshopper has a "Draw Full Names" mode ([McNeel forum, 2023](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/draw-full-names-component-exceptions-slider-names/166142)); the [Bifocals plugin](https://www.food4rhino.com/en/app/bifocals) (2016, old, third party; not re-verified on 2026-10-31, food4rhino returned HTTP 405) labels every component with its full name; McNeel staff note Grasshopper input expressions switched to "x" partly because "there is a mode which shows the full name of the input" ([McNeel forum, 2016, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-for-mac-available-for-testing/28066/71)). Bifocals' author writes "beginner Grasshopper users are easily confused by following text labels", and a new user finds "full names much easier and quicker to use and understand (like many fellow students)" ([McNeel forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/grasshopper-2-ui-wishes/97749)). Since Flow's nodes are the program's own commands, Flow would show the command's menu name and full port names by default, offer a compact mode for experts, and keep both names searchable. Bifocals draws those labels by "wrapping each individual Grasshopper component in a transparent group", and the groups stay in the file for colleagues without the plug-in ([McNeel forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/how-to-remove-component-names/219982)), one reason for Flow to draw full names itself rather than add canvas objects.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** -

<a id="port-08"></a>

#### PORT-08 Reset to default and show changed values
Values that differ from the default are marked, and one action restores the default. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/parms.html) shows "values different from the default as bold", resets one parameter with Ctrl+click and all of a node's with "Revert to Factory Defaults". Grasshopper users ask for a slider "reset" command with a "default value" property, even a global reset for a whole definition ([McNeel forum, 2019, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/reset-step-nudge-slider/82351)). Flow would put a dot on inline fields and value nodes that differ from their default, offer "Reset to default" on right-click (and Backspace while hovering), and "Reset all inputs" in the node menu, each as one canvas-history step. Natron's node panels have a "Hide parameters without modifications" button, "a quick way to find what parameters were modified in a node" ([Natron 2.5 docs](https://natron.readthedocs.io/en/rb-2.5/guide/thenodes-common-properties.html)).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** PORT-01

<a id="port-09"></a>

#### PORT-09 Hide unused and advanced ports
Rarely used inputs stay out of sight until needed, so nodes are small and readable. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/editing.html) hides unconnected sockets with Ctrl-H and lets group panels be "Closed by Default" ([groups](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/groups.html)); [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/right-side-panel) collects advanced inputs in an "ADVANCED INPUTS" section, with a setting to [always show advanced widgets](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/settings/lite-graph). A Blender user calls hide-unused-sockets "a single button press instant node tree declutter" and prefers it to collapsing ([devtalk, 2022](https://devtalk.blender.org/t/feedback-wanted-on-node-layout-changes-to-match-the-node-grid/23904/18)). Flow would let commands mark inputs as advanced, shown behind a "More" row on the node, add Ctrl+H to hide unconnected ports in a selection, and never hide a port whose value differs from its default.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** PORT-08 (changed-value marking)

<a id="port-10"></a>

#### PORT-10 Add and remove inputs on the node
Nodes that take a variable number of things show buttons or a growing slot to add inputs. [Grasshopper](https://developer.rhino3d.com/api/grasshopper/html/T_Grasshopper_Kernel_IGH_VariableParameterComponent.htm) components can "enable variable parameter UI" (the zoomable + and − buttons); [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/features/autogrow-inputs) autogrow inputs add a slot "as soon as you connect the last free one" (Batch Images up to 50); [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/wire.html) Merge takes many inputs. Discoverability is the issue: a Grasshopper user found adding inputs by zooming "a nice discovery after digging for a while" ([McNeel forum, 2023](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/setting-a-solid-building-a-selector-to-choose-a-solid/162665)). Flow would give such commands (merge lists, union, join, text concatenate) a visible "+ add input" row at every zoom, an auto-growing empty last slot, and a "−" on hover.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** WIRE-10

<a id="port-13"></a>

#### PORT-13 Context defaults for empty inputs
An input with nothing connected takes a sensible value from context, and says so. [Blender](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/controls/nodes/groups.html) group sockets can default to Position, Index, Normal or Self Object when unconnected; [Unity Shader Graph Port Bindings](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.shadergraph@17.4/manual/Port-Bindings.html) fill an unconnected port with, for example, the mesh UV or normal. Beginners stall on nodes that "still need input to work" ([Dynamo forum, 2021, old](https://forum.dynamobim.com/t/exercise-goes-wrong/58997)); a context default removes that step for the obvious case. Flow would let command inputs declare a context default such as the current selection, the flow's layer or the active view, show it in the inline field in italics ("selection"), and show the real source in "Explain this number".
**Status:** PARTLY: "sources (a layer, the selection, a saved view, ...)" · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (sources) · **Depends on:** PORT-01; lineage

### Node state, errors and debugging (STATE)

13 features: 2 EXISTS, 1 PARTLY, 10 MISSING.

<a id="state-01"></a>

#### STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes
Each node shows how its last run went: normal, info, warning (it ran, but something is off) or error (it made nothing), and the worst state wins. [Grasshopper 1](https://grasshopper3d.com/profiles/blogs/objects-explained) turns a component orange for a warning and red for an error, and "the error colour takes precedence over the warning colour" (McNeel blog, 2009, old). [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires.md) draws a red, yellow or blue bar under the node, ranked "Error > Warning > Info > Preview". [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/badges.html) adds error and warning badges that can be drawn large "to make important badges (such as errors) more prominent and easier to spot zoomed out". [Blender 4.x](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/inspection.html) puts a warning icon in the node title, and [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/development/core-concepts/nodes) marks the failing input in red. Users need more than colour: a 2026 plugin post points out that the red component is often "just the first one downstream to notice bad data from several steps upstream" ([Traceback, third party](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/traceback-grasshopper-error-explainer-root-cause-tracer/221202)). Flow would give every node a state strip and icon (never colour alone), keep it readable when zoomed out, put it in the node's accessible name and expose it to the test bridge.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** commands that report failures as data ("every command runs from data"); accessible names; the test bridge

<a id="state-02"></a>

#### STATE-02 Plain-language message with a suggested fix
The node's message says what went wrong in the user's own terms (which items, which input) and offers next steps as buttons. Grasshopper 1 shows message balloons with errors, warnings and remarks, which users even script against to collect ([forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/is-it-possible-to-get-error-balloon-message-contents/214726)). In [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires.md) "Any Node that is Yellow will also have a tooltip above the Name", and the primer tells users to "examine the upstream Nodes". In [Blender 4.x](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/inspection.html) "Hovering over the warning icon shows the error message". The [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/right-side-panel.md) Errors tab goes further and pairs missing node packs and models with install and download buttons. Raw messages fail users: [Sverchok](https://nortikin.github.io/sverchok/docs/tree_evaluation_system.html) admits that a Python error "Sometimes ... can help to understand what is wrong but sometimes not", and a trainer preparing an Autodesk course got only "Internal Error Pleas Report: Dereferencing a non-pointer" ([forum, 2018, old, third party](https://forum.robotsinarchitecture.org/index.php?msg=2043)). Dynamo 2.6 added in-depth help for the [30 most common error messages](https://dynamobim.org/dynamo-core-2-6-release/), "collected and scraped from the Github Issues registry and the Dynamo Forum" (vendor blog, 2020, old), and 2.14 put a "Learn more" link into a warning ([release notes](https://github.com/DynamoDS/Dynamo/wiki/Release-Notes)). Flow would keep one message catalogue and have each command write messages from one template (what happened, how many items, why, how to fix) with a "Learn more" link and up to three actions, such as "Select the 3 lots in the drawing", "Show the upstream node" and "Open help".
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes; Show and Select pills; LEARN help pages

<a id="state-03"></a>

#### STATE-03 "Why is this empty?" upstream trace
When a port shows 0 items or empty values, one click walks upstream to the first node where the count dropped to zero or the empty values appeared, and shows what that node received. [Dynamo](https://dynamobim.org/dynamo-core-2-15-release/) 2.15's Graph Node Manager has an Outputs filter for nodes that return null values and empty lists. [Sverchok](https://nortikin.github.io/sverchok/docs/tree_evaluation_system.html) raises a NoDataError "when previous node does not have any data in output sockets". The Grasshopper plugin [Traceback](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/traceback-grasshopper-error-explainer-root-cause-tracer/221202) traces a red component back to its real cause (third party, 2026). The pain is common: "I love grasshopper, but not the bugs on empty branches" ran to 24 replies and 1,044 views ([forum tag page, 2024 to 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/tag/null), [thread](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/i-love-grasshopper-but-not-the-bugs-on-empty-branches/181692)). Another user asked for "a way to retrieve the null index" after a failure ([forum, 2022](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/replace-member-error-solution-exception/147030)). Flow would extend "Explain this number" to the count 0: the tree would show the chain of counts (212, 212, 0), stop at the node that dropped everything, and quote its condition and the values it actually saw, for example an attribute key spelled differently on this layer.
**Status:** PARTLY: "Every result carries lineage; "Explain this number" opens a tree from any figure (... flow port)" and "Output ports show count badges" · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (Explain this number) · **Depends on:** Explain this number; count badges; STATE-02 Plain-language message with a suggested fix

<a id="state-04"></a>

#### STATE-04 Explain this number (lineage tree)
From any figure, Flow opens a tree down to the shapes and the node that made them; this is the root of most debugging in Flow. Other programs ship only parts of it. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/data/data-mapping/data-item-linking/) keeps item lineage, where "Each item links back to the items in the previous nodes that generated it". [Unreal Blueprints](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-debugger-in-unreal-engine) keeps an Execution Trace, a list of the nodes that ran. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/info.html)'s node info window shows a node's counts and the attributes it added. Grasshopper has no built-in equivalent; a 2026 third-party plugin was written because the red component is usually not the cause ([Traceback](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/traceback-grasshopper-error-explainer-root-cause-tracer/221202)). Flow already has this. It should become the hub that STATE-02 actions, STATE-03 empty traces and the problems panel open into, so that every message offers "Explain" as an action.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="state-05"></a>

#### STATE-05 Problems panel with counts and locate
One place lists every error, warning and info in the flow, with a counter that jumps to each node in turn. [Dynamo](https://dynamobim.org/dynamo-core-2-15-release/) 2.15 added a Graph Footer with Error, Warning and Info counters "which you can click to cycle through, focusing in on those nodes", and a Graph Node Manager that can copy messages and "export this data to CSV or JSON". The [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/right-side-panel.md) Errors tab groups problems by type, with "Locate node on canvas". In [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/handling-errors), "When a node logs an error, it will appear in the Debug sidebar". Grasshopper users build this themselves: one shared a script that "scans entire canvas, including inside clusters" for component messages ([forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/is-it-possible-to-get-error-balloon-message-contents/214726)). Flow would add a chip in the flow window ("2 errors · 5 warnings") that cycles and frames each node using the existing fit (F), and a docked list showing node, message and sub-flow path, with filters and copy. Errors inside linked flows and flows used as tools would be included.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes; STATE-02 Plain-language message; fit (F, Home, A); sub-flows

<a id="state-06"></a>

#### STATE-06 Warnings roll up into groups, sub-flows and tools
A collapsed container shows the worst state of anything inside it, so problems cannot hide. In [Blender 4.3](https://developer.blender.org/docs/release_notes/4.3/geometry_nodes/), "By default, all warning types are propagated to the parent node groups", and a "Warning Propagation" setting lets asset authors stop internal ones. The [Blender manual](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/output/warning.html) adds that the warnings are shown in the modifier's Warnings panel, so people who never open the group still see them. Grasshopper clusters lack this. Users ask for scanners that look "inside clusters" ([forum, 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/is-it-possible-to-get-error-balloon-message-contents/214726)) and report that empty branches inside clusters behave unexpectedly, which McNeel fixed only in 2026 ([forum, 2024 to 2026](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/i-love-grasshopper-but-not-the-bugs-on-empty-branches/181692)). Flow would show the worst inner state, with a count ("2 warnings inside"), on a collapsed sub-flow node, a group title, a linked flow and a flow used as a tool, and clicking it would open the path to the node. When a flow runs as a tool from the rail, its messages would appear where the tool runs.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (sub-flows; flow as a tool) · **Depends on:** STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes; sub-flows; groups; import as a link; flow as a tool

<a id="state-07"></a>

#### STATE-07 Out-of-date marker in manual mode
When the flow is not running automatically, any edit marks the changed node and everything downstream as stale until the next run. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/build/understand-workflows/understand-executions/understand-dirty-nodes.md) calls a node dirty when it "executed successfully in the past, but whose output n8n now considers stale or unreliable". It shows "a different-colored border and a yellow triangle in place of the previous green tick symbol", and a tooltip explains why. n8n built it because "any change could have an effect on the items produced and thus can have an effect on downstream nodes" ([n8n pull request #11739, 2024](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/pull/11739)). No CAD node editor studied ships this marker. Flow already has manual (Ctrl+Enter) and delay modes and a dependency graph. Flow would hatch the state strip of stale nodes, grey their count badges, tag stale results on the flow's layer through the Show pill, and clear the marks on the next run. A new user would then never mistake an old result for the current one.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (Run modes: manual, delay) · **Depends on:** run modes; dependency graph ("Live values"); count badges

<a id="state-08"></a>

#### STATE-08 Per-node error policy: stop, skip failed items, error output
Each node can say what happens when some of its items fail: stop the run, skip the failed items and go on, or send the failures down a separate output. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/workflows/components/nodes/) has an On Error setting with "Stop Workflow", "Continue" and "Continue (using error output)". In [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/handling-errors), a Catch node "can be used to create a flow to handle it". [Sverchok](https://nortikin.github.io/sverchok/docs/tree_evaluation_system.html) stops only the nodes downstream of an error, so "errors in detached groups of nodes won't effect each other execution". The demand shows in n8n's own fix for a node where "there is a bug that causes the execution to stop once an item fails" ([pull request #9890, 2024](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/pull/9890)). In urban design, one bad parcel should not sink a 400-parcel run. Flow would default list-processing commands to skipping failed items with a warning ("3 of 400 skipped") and offer an optional "failed" output port carrying the failed inputs and reasons, ready to select in the drawing. There would be no "use last valid data" option (see do-not-copy).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes; wire strokes for lists; Show and Select pills

<a id="state-09"></a>

#### STATE-09 Disable and bypass
Disable stops a node and what depends on it; bypass passes the input through unchanged. [Houdini](https://www.sidefx.com/docs/houdini/network/flags.html)'s bypass flag "disables the node, making it pass its input geometry through to the output unchanged". [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/development/core-concepts/nodes) separates Never ("as if it's been deleted") from Bypass, where "subsequent nodes can still try to obtain data that hasn't been processed by this node". Grasshopper draws a disabled component "in a dull grey" ([BIM Corner, 2021, old, third party](https://bimcorner.com/grasshopper-for-beginners-vol-2/)). In [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires.md), freezing a node "also freezes the nodes that are downstream of it". Users want this to test one part in isolation: a ComfyUI user asked for a way "to check output of just one node without activating the rest connected nodes" ([GitHub discussion, 2023](https://github.com/Comfy-Org/ComfyUI/discussions/1024)). Flow already has D disable and B bypass. The STATE-01 strip should show these two states differently from errors, so that a bypassed node is never mistaken for a failed one.
**Status:** EXISTS · **Size:** - · **Value:** 4 · **Kind:** - · **Depends on:** -

<a id="state-10"></a>

#### STATE-10 Author-defined check node (custom warnings)
Flow authors can add a node that raises their own info, warning or error message when a condition holds, so tools built from flows guard their inputs. The [Blender 4.x](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/output/warning.html) Warning node "allows node groups to communicate expectations, assumptions, or error conditions, such as required input ranges, missing data, or unsupported configurations", with Info, Warning and Error types. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/flow-logic/error-handling/)'s Stop and Error node lets authors "force executions to fail under your chosen circumstances". Blender's [4.3 release notes](https://developer.blender.org/docs/release_notes/4.3/geometry_nodes/) present the node as a way to add "custom warnings to node groups", alongside a propagation setting that keeps internal warnings away from end users. Flow already lets a flow become a tool for people who never open it. Flow would add a Check node that takes a condition expression, a message with placeholders ("Site boundary must be closed; 2 open curves found") and a severity. Its message would appear on the tool node, in the problems panel and where the tool runs.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** S · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** COMPLETION (flow as a tool; expressions) · **Depends on:** expressions; STATE-01; STATE-06 Warnings roll up; flow as a tool

<a id="state-12"></a>

#### STATE-12 Run trace on the canvas
During and after a run, each node shows whether it ran, was skipped, failed or was never reached, and the node running now is marked. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/build/understand-workflows/understand-executions/understand-dirty-nodes.md) gives executed nodes a green tick. The [Unreal Blueprint](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-debugger-in-unreal-engine) Debugger's "Execution Trace stack shows a list of nodes executed with the most recent node listed at the top". [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/development/core-concepts/nodes) shows a running state "when a node is executing". [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/creating-nodes/status) nodes show a coloured dot or ring with short text such as "connected". ComfyUI users install a third-party monitor just to see "Current node execution status" ([ComfyUI-TaskMonitor, GitHub, third party](https://github.com/hmwl/ComfyUI-TaskMonitor)). Flow would mark nodes as ran, skipped (disabled, bypassed, or not reached because something upstream failed) or failed after each run, briefly brighten wires that carried data, and pulse the node that is running next to the existing cancel. Per-node timings belong to PERF.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 3 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes; run modes; cancel

<a id="state-13"></a>

#### STATE-13 Run history with per-node data
Past runs are kept with their inputs, counts and messages, so a user can see what changed between runs or reopen an earlier state. [n8n](https://docs.n8n.io/data/) lets users "Review past workflow runs to see the data that passed through each node", and [Debug in editor](https://docs.n8n.io/workflows/executions/debug/) copies a failed run's data into the workflow for "debugging data from failed production executions". The [ComfyUI](https://docs.comfy.org/interface/job-history.md) Job History lists completed and failed jobs, with "Open as workflow in a new tab". The [Node-RED](https://nodered.org/docs/user-guide/editor/sidebar/debug) Debug sidebar keeps a log but "can only show the 100 most recent messages". The demand in design tools is about reusing earlier work: "Is there a way to feed previously calculated results into the Opossum component?" ([forum, 2026, third-party plugin](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/optimization-with-opossum/220721)). Flow would keep the last N runs of each flow: slider and source values, count per port and messages, but not full geometry. Any port would offer "Compare with previous run", and an old run's input values could be restored.
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** L · **Value:** 2 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** count badges; STATE-02 messages; canvas history; .urbflow saving

<a id="state-14"></a>

#### STATE-14 Breakpoints and step-through
Execution pauses at a chosen node so the user can inspect values, then steps forward one node or message at a time. In [Unreal Blueprints](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/blueprint-debugger-in-unreal-engine), "Breakpoints are markers that can be placed on Blueprint graph nodes" that pause just before the node runs, watches keep pin values, and the trace updates "as you step through the graph". The [Node-RED Flow Debugger](https://github.com/node-red/node-red-debugger) plugin can "set breakpoints on node inputs or outputs", "pause the runtime manually or when a message arrives at a breakpoint" and "step forward individual messages". Node-RED users asked for breakpoints from 2018 until the team answered "A flow debugger is on the roadmap" in 2020 ([forum, 2018 to 2020, old](https://discourse.nodered.org/t/node-red-breakpoints/2073)). In a dataflow CAD editor, the hover peek and previews cover most of this. The real gap is inside loop containers. Flow would add "Pause after each pass" on a loop, with Step (one pass), Continue and Cancel, and show the ports of the current pass. Rhino 8's Script component can be debugged inside Grasshopper: a click beside a line number sets a breakpoint, Debug runs to it, "Step Over: executes current line and moves on the next line", and a counter such as "Run Script (2 of 11)" shows which run is being debugged ([McNeel developer guide](https://developer.rhino3d.com/guides/scripting/scripting-component/)); a tester wrote that "The tabbed debugging is a really nice feature for debugging handoffs between multiple components" ([McNeel forum, 2023](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/rhino-8-grasshopper-script-editor-design-feedback/163513)).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** L · **Value:** 2 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** containers (loop with pass count); STATE-12 Run trace; cancel

### Data shapes (DATA)

9 features: 0 EXISTS, 3 PARTLY, 6 MISSING.

<a id="data-01"></a>

#### DATA-01 Matching rule per node, shown on the node
When a node's inputs hold different numbers of items, a matching rule decides how they pair: repeat the last item, stop at the shortest, cycle, or every combination. The rule is chosen per node and is visible. [Grasshopper 1](https://developer.rhino3d.com/guides/grasshopper/gh-algorithms-and-data-structures/data-structures/) defaults to longest list: "GH reuses the last item on the shorter list and matches it with the next items in the longer list". Its cross reference gives a length "equal to the multiplication product of the length of input lists" (McNeel guide, 2024). [Dynamo](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/5_essential_nodes_and_concepts/5-4_designing-with-lists/1-whats-a-list.md) offers shortest, longest and cross product lacing from the right-click menu, with a "Lacing Icon" [on the node](https://primer2.dynamobim.org/4_nodes_and_wires.md). [Sverchok](https://nortikin.github.io/sverchok/docs/nodes/list_main/match.html) has Short, Cycle, Repeat and X-Ref. Silent repetition confuses users: "the last item is duplicated repeatedly" ([forum, 2020, old](https://discourse.mcneel.com/t/ghpython-multiple-inputs-are-all-given-same-list-length-when-input-to-functions/101324)), and a commenter on McNeel's first explanation wrote "People have difficulties to explain this concept" ([blog comment, 2010, old](https://www.grasshopper3d.com/profiles/blogs/data-matching-explained)). Flow would show a matching chip on multi-input nodes ("1 : 1", "repeat last", "all × all"). When counts differ, the chip would turn into a warning ("12 parcels, 1 height: height repeated 11 times"), and cross reference would warn above a size limit. In GenerativeComponents "The default matching of two lists is pair-by-pair", and a node's Replication Style switches it to "an exhaustive set of matches between the two sets of values" ([GC Help v10, Replication](https://docs.bentley.com/LiveContent/web/OpenBuildings%20GenerativeComponents%20Help-v10/en/GUID-F20C6836-2DEB-675F-FB7E-C9A3A3717C1E.html)).
**Status:** MISSING · **Size:** M · **Value:** 5 · **Kind:** NEW · **Depends on:** wire strokes (one item, list, list of lists); count badges; STATE-01 Error and warning states on nodes

<a id="data-02"></a>

#### DATA-02 Viewer node and pinned peek
